This lets every rank materialize its\n//   own slice of a globally defined random array without allocating the whole\n//   array anywhere.\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid fillRange(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal, const index_t first,\n               const index_t last) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        const double v = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n        if (i >= first && i < last) {\n            A[i - first] = v;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrixLocal\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix, encoded in compressed sparse row (CSR) format. Identical in\n//   outcome to a serial CSR construction of the whole matrix, but only the\n//   column indices of the locally owned row block [rowFirst, rowLast) are\n//   stored. The (small) global row delimiter array is kept on every rank so\n//   that ranks agree on the global nonzero numbering.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of the locally owned nonzeros\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding global indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements (global)\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   rowFirst:      first row owned by this rank\n//   rowLast:       one past the last row owned by this rank\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrixLocal(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n,\n                           const index_t dim, const index_t rowFirst, const index_t rowLast) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        const bool owned = (i >= rowFirst && i < rowLast);\n        // Global nonzero index of the first locally owned nonzero\n        const index_t nnzFirst = rowDelimiters[rowFirst < dim ? rowFirst : 0];\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                if (owned) {\n                    cols[nnzAssigned - nnzFirst] = j;\n                }\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n            const auto col = cols[j];\n            t += val[j] * vec[col];\n        }\n        out[i] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//   offset: global index of the first element (for diagnostic output)\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size,\n                   const index_t offset) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n        const double ref = reference[i];\n        const double res = result[i];\n        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n            // For very small values, check absolute error\n            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", i + offset,\n                       ref, res);\n                return false;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // Check relative error\n            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i + offset, ref, res, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0;\n    int numRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);\n\n    index_t numRows = 1024;\n    index_t sparsity = 10;\n    index_t iterations = 10;\n    double maxVal = 1.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments (identical on every rank)\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numRows = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-m\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            maxVal = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if This lets every rank materialize its\n//   own slice of a globally defined random array without allocating the whole\n//   array anywhere.\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid fillRange(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal, const index_t first,\n               const index_t last) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        const double v = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n        if (i >= first && i < last) {\n            A[i - first] = v;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrixLocal\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix, encoded in compressed sparse row (CSR) format. Identical in\n//   outcome to a serial CSR construction of the whole matrix, but only the\n//   column indices of the locally owned row block [rowFirst, rowLast) are\n//   stored. The (small) global row delimiter array is kept on every rank so\n//   that ranks agree on the global nonzero numbering.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of the locally ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrixLocal(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n,\n                           const index_t dim, const index_t rowFirst, const index_t rowLast) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = rowLast);\n        // Global nonzero index of the first locally owned nonzero\n        const index_t nnzFirst = rowDelimiters[rowFirst < dim ? rowFirst : 0];\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                if (owned) {\n                    cols[nnzAssigned - nnzFirst] = j;\n                }\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const                ref, res);\n                return false;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // Check relative error\n            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nusing index_t = uint32_t;\n\n// Constants\nconstexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;\n\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n            const auto col = cols[j];\n            t += val[j] * vec[col];\n        }\n        out[i] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: fillRange\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Distributed-memory variant of fill(): draws the same sequence of n random\n//   values as fill() would, but only stores the values with global indices in\n//   [first, last) into A[0..last-first). This lets every rank materialize its\n//   own slice of a globally defined random array without allocating the whole\n//   array anywhere.\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid fillRange(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal, const index_t first,\n               const index_t last) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        const double v = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n        if (i >= first && i < last) {\n            A[i - first] = v;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrixLocal\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix, encoded in compressed sparse row (CSR) format. Identical in\n//   outcome to a serial CSR construction of the whole matrix, but only the\n//   column indices of the locally owned row block [rowFirst, rowLast) are\n//   stored. The (small) global row delimiter array is kept on every rank so\n//   that ranks agree on the global nonzero numbering.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of the locally owned nonzeros\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding global indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements (global)\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   rowFirst:      first row owned by this rank\n//   rowLast:       one past the last row owned by this rank\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrixLocal(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n,\n                           const index_t dim, const index_t rowFirst, const index_t rowLast) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        const bool owned = (i >= rowFirst && i < rowLast);\n        // Global nonzero index of the first locally owned nonzero\n        const index_t nnzFirst = rowDelimiters[rowFirst < dim ? rowFirst : 0];\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                if (owned) {\n                    cols[nnzAssigned - nnzFirst] = j;\n                }\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n            const auto col = cols[j];\n            t += val[j] * vec[col];\n        }\n        out[i] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//   offset: global index of the first element (for diagnostic output)\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size,\n                   const index_t offset) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n        const double ref = reference[i];\n        const double res = result[i];\n        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n            // For very small values, check absolute error\n            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", i + offset,\n                       ref, res);\n                return false;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // Check relative error\n            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i + offset, ref, res, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0;\n    int numRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);\n\n    index_t numRows = on every rank)\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numRows = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if This lets every rank materialize its\n//   own slice of a globally defined random array without allocating the whole\n//   array anywhere.\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid fillRange(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal, const index_t first,\n               const index_t last) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        const double v = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n        if (i >= first && i < last) {\n            A[i - first] = v;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrixLocal\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix, encoded in compressed sparse row (CSR) format. Identical in\n//   outcome to a serial CSR construction of the whole matrix, but only the\n//   column indices of the locally owned row block [rowFirst, rowLast) are\n//   stored. The (small) global row delimiter array is kept on every rank so\n//   that ranks agree on the global nonzero numbering.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of the locally This lets every rank materialize its\n//   own slice of a globally defined random array without allocating the whole\n//   array anywhere.\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid fillRange(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal, const index_t first,\n               const index_t last) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        const double v = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n        if (i >= first && i < last) {\n            A[i - first] = v;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrixLocal\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix, encoded in compressed sparse row (CSR) format. Identical in\n//   outcome to a serial CSR construction of the whole matrix, but only the\n//   column indices of the locally owned row block [rowFirst, rowLast) are\n//   stored. The (small) global row delimiter array is kept on every rank so\n//   that ranks agree on the global nonzero numbering.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of the locally owned nonzeros\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding global indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements (global)\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   rowFirst:      first row owned by this rank\n//   rowLast:       one past the last row owned by this rank\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrixLocal(std::vector<index_t>& cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n,\n                           const index_t dim, const index_t rowFirst, const index_t rowLast) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        const bool owned = (i >= rowFirst && i < rowLast);\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                if (owned) {\n                    cols.push_back(j);\n                }\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n            const auto col = cols[j];\n            t += val[j] * vec[col];\n        }\n        out[i] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//   offset: global index of the first element (for diagnostic output)\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size,\n                   const index_t offset) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n        const double ref = reference[i];\n        const double res = result[i];\n        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n            // For very small values, check absolute error\n            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", i + offset,\n                       ref, res);\n                return false;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // Check relative error\n            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i + offset, ref, res, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0;\n    int numRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);\n\n    index_t numRows = 1024;\n    index_t sparsity = 10;\n    index_t iterations = 10;\n    double maxVal = 1.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments (identical on every rank)\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numRows = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-m\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            maxVal = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Calculate number of non-zero elements\n    const index_t nItems = (numRows * numRows) / sparsity;\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Matrix size: %u x %u\\n\", numRows, numRows);\n        printf(\"Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\\n\", sparsity);\n        printf(\"Non-zero elements: %u (%.2f%% sparse)\\n\",\n               nItems, 100.0 * (1.0 - static_cast<double>(nItems) / (numRows * numRows)));\n        printf(\"Iterations: %u\\n\", iterations);\n        printf(\"Max value: %.2f\\n\", maxVal);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n        printf(\"MPI ranks: %d\\n\", numRanks);\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Row-block decomposition: each rank owns a contiguous range of rows\n    // and the corresponding nonzeros. The dense input vector is needed in\n    // full by every rank (columns are spread over the whole matrix), and it\n    // is identical on all ranks, so it is replicated instead of communicated.\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    std::vector<int> rowCounts(numRanks);\n    std::vector<int> rowOffsets(numRanks);\n    {\n        const index_t base = numRows / static_cast<index_t>(numRanks);\n        const index_t rem = numRows % static_cast<index_t>(numRanks);\n        index_t off = 0;\n        for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {\n            const index_t cnt = base + (static_cast<index_t>(r) < rem ? 1u : 0u);\n            rowCounts[r] = static_cast<int>(cnt);\n            rowOffsets[r] = static_cast<int>(off);\n            off += cnt;\n        }\n    }\n    const index_t rowFirst = static_cast<index_t>(rowOffsets[rank]);\n    const index_t localRows = static_cast<index_t>(rowCounts[rank]);\n    const index_t rowLast = rowFirst + localRows;\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Initializing data structures...\\n\");\n    }\n\n    // Global row delimiters (dim+1 indices, small) are replicated; column\n    // indices and matrix values are stored only for the locally owned rows.\n    std::vector<index_t> h_rowDelimiters(numRows + 1);\n    std::vector<double> h_vec(numRows);   // Dense vector (replicated)\n    std::vector<double> h_out(localRows); // Local part of the output vector\n\n    // Determine the locally owned nonzero range and the local column indices.\n    // The generation stream is reproduced identically on every rank, so all\n    // ranks agree on the global matrix without any communication.\n    std::vector<index_t> h_cols;\n    h_cols.reserve(static_cast<size_t>(nItems) / static_cast<size_t>(numRanks) + 1024);\n    initRandomMatrixLocal(h_cols, h_rowDelimiters.data(), nItems, numRows, rowFirst, rowLast);\n\n    const index_t nnzFirst = h_rowDelimiters[rowFirst];\n    const index_t nnzLast = h_rowDelimiters[rowLast];\n    const index_t localNnz = nnzLast - nnzFirst;\n\n    // Reproduce the global random streams for the dense vector and the matrix\n    // values. rand() starts out as if srand(1) had been called, which is the\n    // state the serial code relies on for these two arrays.\n    std::vector<double> h_val(localNnz);\n    srand(1);\n    fill(h_vec.data(), numRows, maxVal);\n    fillRange(h_val.data(), nItems, maxVal, nnzFirst, nnzLast);\n\n    // Local CSR row pointers, rebased to the local value/column arrays\n    std::vector<index_t> localRowPtr(localRows + 1);\n    for (index_t i = 0; i <= localRows; ++i) {\n        localRowPtr[i] = h_rowDelimiters[rowFirst + i] - nnzFirst;\n    }\n\n    // For validation, compute reference solution (each rank for its own rows)\n    std::vector<double> h_reference;\n    if (validate) {\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            printf(\"Computing reference solution...\\n\");\n        }\n        h_reference.resize(localRows);\n        spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), localRowPtr.data(), h_vec.data(), localRows,\n                h_reference.data());\n          h_out.data());\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    long localMs = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start).count();\n\n    // The benchmark time is the slowest rank's time\n    long durationMs = 0;\n    MPI_Reduce(&localMs, &durationMs, 1, MPI_LONG, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", durationMs);\n\n        // Calculate performance metrics\n        const double gflops = (2.0 * nItems * iterations) / (durationMs / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n        const double avgTime = durationMs / static_cast<double>(iterations);\n\n        printf(\"Average time per iteration: %.3f ms\\n\", avgTime);\n        printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    }\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        // Assemble the full output vector on rank 0\n        std::vector<double> h_outGlobal(rank == 0 ? numRows : 0);\n        MPI_Gatherv(h_out.data(), static_cast<int>(localRows), MPI_DOUBLE, h_outGlobal.data(),\n                    rowCounts.data(), rowOffsets.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            print_results(h_outGlobal, \"OutputVector\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        }\n        const int localValid =\n            verifyResults(h_reference.data(), h_out.data(), localRows, rowFirst) ? 1 : 0;\n        int valid = 0;\n        MPI_Allreduce(&localValid, &valid, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            printf(valid ? \"Validation: PASSED\\n\" : \"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n        }\n        MPI_Finalize();\n        return valid ? 0 : 1;\n    }\n\n    MPI_Finalize();\n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":369,"oldLines":7,"newStart":369,"newLines":11,"lines":["
